SBD /No. 9 / 1975 . — It is hereby notified for general 
information that in pursuance of clause ( c ) of sub -sec 
tion ( 1 ) of Section 25 of the State Bank of India ( Sub 
sidiary Banks ) Act , 1959 ( 38 of 1959 ) , the State Bank 
of India , in consultation with the Reserve Bank of India , 
hereby nominates Dr S . V . Govindarajan , Serpentine Road , 
Kumara Park East , Baurgalore- 20 , as a Director of the 
State Bank of Mysore for a term of three years from 
the 28th May 1975 to the 27th May 1978 (inclusive ) 
in place of Shri M . Mallaraj Urs , who will cease to be 
a Director from today ,
